Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 subclass가 아니라 Shipment에 속한 route segment로 모델링한다. 예: DeliveryLeg.parent_shipment_ref 또는 Shipment.legs를 두고, fulfills 관계는 전체 Shipment에만 유지한다. Leg 상태는 구간 완료 상태로 별도 정의한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 정합성을 제공하여 TMS/OMS/WMS 상태와 관계를 통합하는 목적. Source finding context: 하나의 주문 출하가 여러 구간을 거치거나 중간 허브 구간 상태를 추적할 때 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급된다. Source finding context: 구간 완료와 전체 배송 완료, 구간 운송과 주문 충족이 같은 의미 계층으로 섞여 상태 매핑과 주문 충족 판단이 부정확해질 수 있다. Source finding context: 배송 구간이라는 부분 개념을 전체 출하 이동의 특수한 종류로 분류했다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 화물 경로의 한 구간으로 정의된다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 is_a: Shipment로 선언되어 전체 Shipment의 의미와 관계를 상속한다. Source finding context: Shipment는 Order를 fulfills하는 관계의 출발점이므로 DeliveryLeg도 주문 충족 주체처럼 해석될 수 있다.
  - affected purpose: 화물·배송 개념의 정합성을 제공하여 TMS/OMS/WMS 상태와 관계를 통합하는 목적.
  - failure condition: 하나의 주문 출하가 여러 구간을 거치거나 중간 허브 구간 상태를 추적할 때 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급된다.
  - impact: 구간 완료와 전체 배송 완료, 구간 운송과 주문 충족이 같은 의미 계층으로 섞여 상태 매핑과 주문 충족 판단이 부정확해질 수 있다.
  - root hypothesis: 배송 구간이라는 부분 개념을 전체 출하 이동의 특수한 종류로 분류했다.

  - materiality: 이 문제는 TMS/OMS/WMS 간 화물·배송 상태와 관계를 정합적으로 통합하려는 목적을 약화시킨다. 하나의 주문 출하가 여러 구간을 거칠 때 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment 의미를 상속하면 구간 완료가 전체 배송 완료처럼 보이고, 구간 운송이 주문 충족 주체처럼 해석되어 상태 매핑과 fulfillment 판단이 부정확해질 수 있다.
  - root cause: 시작점은 배송 구간이라는 부분 개념을 전체 출하 이동의 특수한 종류로 분류한 것이다. DeliveryLeg는 경로의 한 구간으로 정의되지만 is_a: Shipment로 선언되어 Shipment의 전체 이동 의미와 관계를 상속하게 되므로, 이후의 상태·관계 혼동이 이 타입 모델링 오류에서 파생된다.
  - causal path: 근거 흐름은 DeliveryLeg가 화물 경로의 한 구간으로 정의된 데서 시작한다. 그런데 이를 Shipment의 하위 유형으로 두면서 전체 Shipment의 의미를 상속하고, Shipment가 Order를 fulfills하는 관계의 출발점이기 때문에 DeliveryLeg도 주문을 충족하는 주체처럼 해석될 수 있다. 이 경로가 구간 완료, 전체 배송 완료, 주문 충족 상태가 같은 의미 계층에서 뒤섞이는 문제를 만든다.
  - action: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 subclass에서 제거하고 Shipment.legs 또는 DeliveryLeg.parent_shipment_ref 같은 포함·연결 관계로 모델링해야 한다. fulfills 관계는 전체 Shipment에만 유지하고, DeliveryLeg의 상태는 leg-specific 완료 또는 운송 상태로 별도 정의해야 한다. 이 변경은 다음 단계 전에 닫아야 하는 구조적 전제이며, 구간 상태와 전체 출하/주문 충족 상태의 해석 경계를 먼저 고정해야 이후 통합 매핑이 안정된다.

Shipment.total_weight는 normalized_weight 합계인지 원천 측정값인지 명시하고, 가능하면 source weights와 calculated_at/source_basis를 함께 둔다. dims도 length/width/height 각각 value+unit으로 분해한다.   - impact: 동일한 total_weight 값이 어떤 단위의 합인지 알 수 없어 운송사, 창고, ERP 간 물류 수치 매핑과 검증이 신뢰하기 어렵다. / 동일 필드의 숫자가 시스템마다 다른 의미를 가져 기존 데이터와 새 데이터의 비교, 합산, 요율 계산 연속성이 약해집니다.
  - root hypothesis: The unit model is not evolvable because measurement unit is not part of the measurement value concept.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md Sku.weight note`, `materialized-input.md Sku.dims note`, `materialized-input.md Shipment.total_weight note`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: Sku.weight note "창고별 계량 관례(kg 또는 lb)를 그대로 쓴다."; Sku.dims note "단위는 소스 시스템마다 다르다."; Shipment.total_weight note "구성 OrderLine 중량의 합."`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:33-38`, `materialized-input.md:40-49`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-38,40-49`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-017`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-011`, `rel-011`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-011`, `finding-011.cause-001`, `finding-011.cause-002`, `finding-011.cause-003`, `finding-011.cause-004`, `finding-017.cause-001`, `finding-017.cause-002`, `finding-017.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-012`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-005`, `Sku `weight` note: warehouse-specific kg or lb convention is used`, `Sku `dims` note: dimensions are a string and units vary by source system`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-012`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.logic`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-011`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-002`, `rel-012`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because the model is meant to be a common integration basis for WMS/TMS/ERP shipment, inventory, and calculation concepts. If kg/lb or cm/in values can enter the same fields without explicit units and conversion basis, downstream carrier exchange, freight rating, load-limit checks, labels, and validation cannot reliably compare or aggregate those values.
  - root cause: The starting point is that measurement is modeled as a bare number or source-dependent string rather than as a value with unit, source unit authority, and normalization basis. Once the unit is outside the measurement concept, every later aggregate or mapping has to guess the meaning of the value instead of deriving it from the model.
  - causal path: The source model declares Sku.weight as a number using warehouse-specific kg or lb convention, Sku.dims as a string whose unit varies by source system, and Shipment.total_weight as the sum of OrderLine weights without recording unit or conversion criteria. That path allows incompatible source measurements to be combined under one total_weight concept, so existing data and future unit-system additions become hard to compare, migrate, or validate consistently.
  - action: Model weight and dimensions as structured measurements with value and unit, distinguish source units from canonical normalized units, and define conversion rules. Split dimensions into length, width, and height measurements, and make Shipment.total_weight explicit about whether it is a normalized aggregate or source measurement, including calculation unit, calculated_at, source_basis, and preferably the contributing source weights or SKU version basis.

  - issue statement: TrackingEvent.event_type과 Shipment.status 사이의 상태 의미가 통합 기준 안에서 정의되지 않는다. Source finding context: entities.TrackingEvent.attributes.event_type and entities.Shipment.attributes.status Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: TrackingEvent.event_type note "캐리어별 상태 코드. 자사 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다."; integrity_rules "Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type는 각 시스템(OMS/TMS/캐리어)이 독립으로 관리한다." Source finding context: 상태 이름에는 의미 전이가 숨어 있다. 예를 들어 TrackingEvent.delivered가 Shipment.status.delivered와 같은 완료 의미인지, 특정 구간의 도착 이벤트인지, 캐리어별 최종 스캔인지 통합 기준이 판단해 주지 않는다. 이 상태 의미를 연동별로 맡기면 같은 이벤트가 통합마다 다른 Shipment 상태를 만들 수 있다. Source finding context: 캐리어 원천 이벤트와 내부 canonical shipment status를 구분하고, 각 이벤트가 상태 전이를 일으키는 조건과 범위를 기준 문서에 둔다. event_type에는 source_carrier_code/source_event_code를 보존하고, canonical_event_type 또는 status_transition 매핑 테이블을 명시한다. Source finding context: delivered, in_transit, exception 같은 상태의 의미가 연동별로 달라져 배송 완료, 예외 처리, 고객 알림 기준이 불일치할 수 있다. Source finding context: 원천 캐리어 이벤트 의미와 내부 Shipment 상태 의미 사이의 canonical 매핑을 온톨로지 밖 연동 구현으로 위임한다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type은 캐리어별 상태 코드로 정의된다. Source finding context: Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: 주문, 화물, 캐리어 상태는 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 되어 있어 공통 상태 의미가 기준 문서 안에서 닫히지 않는다.
  - affected purpose: 배송 상태 모델과 WMS/TMS/ERP/캐리어 통합 상태 의미를 일관되게 제공하는 목적.
  - failure condition: 캐리어 이벤트를 내부 Shipment.status나 Order.fulfillment_status로 반영하는 연동을 둘 이상 구현할 때 각 연동이 자체 매핑을 만든다.
  - impact: delivered, in_transit, exception 같은 상태의 의미가 연동별로 달라져 배송 완료, 예외 처리, 고객 알림 기준이 불일치할 수 있다.
  - root hypothesis: 원천 캐리어 이벤트 의미와 내부 Shipment 상태 의미 사이의 canonical 매핑을 온톨로지 밖 연동 구현으로 위임한다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `materialized-input.md TrackingEvent.event_type note`, `materialized-input.md integrity_rules 상태 독립 관리`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: TrackingEvent.event_type note "캐리어별 상태 코드. 자사 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다."; integrity_rules "Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type는 각 시스템(OMS/TMS/캐리어)이 독립으로 관리한다."`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-018`, `finding-018.cause-001`, `finding-018.cause-002`, `finding-018.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-013`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004`, `TrackingEvent `event_type` includes exception`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-013`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-013.structure`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: 이 문제는 배송 상태 모델과 WMS/TMS/ERP/캐리어 통합 상태 의미를 일관되게 제공하려는 목적을 직접 약화한다. delivered, in_transit, exception 같은 이름이 캐리어 원천 이벤트인지, 내부 Shipment 완료 상태인지, 고객 알림이나 예외 처리 기준인지 닫힌 기준이 없으면 여러 연동이 각자 매핑을 만들고 동일 이벤트가 서로 다른 완료, 진행, 예외 판단으로 이어진다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 원천 캐리어 이벤트 의미와 내부 Shipment 상태 의미 사이의 canonical 매핑을 온톨로지 밖 연동 구현에 위임한 것이다. TrackingEvent.event_type은 캐리어별 상태 코드로 정의되어 있고 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동이 처리하도록 되어 있으므로, 공통 상태 의미의 권위가 기준 문서 안에 존재하지 않는다.
  - causal path: 인과 경로는 TrackingEvent.event_type이 캐리어별 상태 코드로 남아 있는 데서 시작한다. 이어서 Shipment.status와의 매핑을 각 연동이 맡게 되고, Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type가 각 시스템에서 독립 관리된다는 규칙 때문에 통합 기준 안에서 상태 의미가 닫히지 않는다. 그 결과 TrackingEvent.delivered가 내부 Shipment.status.delivered와 같은 완료인지, 특정 구간 도착 이벤트인지, 캐리어의 최종 스캔인지 연동별로 달라질 수 있다.
  - action: 캐리어 원천 이벤트와 내부 canonical shipment status를 분리해 모델링하고, 각 이벤트가 Shipment.status나 Order.fulfillment_status 전이를 일으키는 조건과 범위를 기준 문서에 명시해야 한다. event_type에는 source_carrier_code와 source_event_code를 보존하고, 별도의 canonical_event_type 또는 status_transition 매핑 테이블을 두어 상태 파생을 연동 구현의 암묵 지식이 아니라 온톨로지의 명시 기준으로 만들어야 한다.

#### Action Candidates
- issue-001: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The deliberation resolution keeps this as a high-severity material inventory defect accepted by all lenses. Because allocation correctness, audit reconstruction, and WMS/ERP reconciliation depend on the missing authority and temporal model, it is a root cause that should be closed before using the ontology as the next-stage integration baseline. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate inventory authority and allocation timing Source finding context: Target: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:81, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:111, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:115. Value authority anchor: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6, statement requires suitability as a WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept standard centered on authority, temporality, units, status models, and logistics operating risk. Source finding context: Nightly ERP-aligned inventory snapshots are misaligned with the ontology's declared purpose as an integration concept standard for inventory authority and time-sensitive allocation. Source finding context: For an integration reference document whose review purpose explicitly centers authority, temporality, and logistics operating risk, making intraday allocation depend on a nightly aggregate accepts a hidden tradeoff: accounting reconciliation convenience overrides operational stock truth at the moment orders are accepted. This weakens the ontology's ability to define which system is authoritative for allocation-time inventory and when a quantity is valid. Source finding context: Separate physical, accounting, and allocatable inventory concepts; add as_of/effective_at timestamps; define the authority for allocation-time availability, and require allocations to reference a current WMS-backed or event-sourced availability record rather than a daily ERP-aligned snapshot. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use the ontology as the WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts, especially authority and temporality. Source finding context: During intraday order allocation, the ontology directs consumers to a daily InventoryAggregate snapshot while operational WMS and accounting ERP quantities may diverge until nightly reconciliation. Source finding context: The reference model can drive oversell, under-allocation, and audit disputes because it does not identify the valid inventory authority or validity time for allocation decisions. Source finding context: The ontology optimizes for nightly ERP reconciliation as the inventory integration authority without modeling allocation-time validity or separating physical, accounting, and allocatable inventory purposes. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is explicitly shared by WMS and ERP, and discrepancies are adjusted to ERP at night. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a nightly snapshot but is used for intraday allocation. Source finding context: Because the review purpose requires authority and temporality suitability for WMS/TMS/ERP integration, this snapshot allocation rule undermines the declared purpose. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate temporal inventory coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: InventoryRecord `quantity_on_hand` note and comment; InventoryAggregate `available_qty` note; integrity rule for allocation Source finding context: Inventory quantities lack effective-time and movement-history coverage despite being used for allocation. Source finding context: Inventory values are time-dependent. The target itself notes that `InventoryRecord` has no `as_of` concept, while allocation reads `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` even for intraday orders although that aggregate is a nightly snapshot. This leaves a coverage gap for historical reconstruction, oversell investigation, reservation accounting, and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: Introduce temporal inventory concepts: `InventorySnapshot` with `as_of`, source system, and scope; `InventoryMovement` or `InventoryTransaction` for receipts, picks, shipments, returns, adjustments; and `Reservation`/`Allocation` with effective time and release/consume events. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Inventory and order fulfillment authority for WMS/TMS/ERP integration. Source finding context: When an order is accepted or audited at a prior point in time, the ontology cannot reconstruct available or on-hand inventory from effective timestamps or movement history. Source finding context: The concept baseline cannot support allocation correctness, dispute analysis, or cross-system reconciliation because only current/nightly quantity surfaces are modeled. Source finding context: Inventory is represented as current/nighly aggregate values without temporal source concepts or movement events. Source finding context: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` is a current quantity with no effective timestamp. Source finding context: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` is only a nightly snapshot but is used for intraday allocation decisions. Source finding context: The model lacks inventory movement, reservation, and adjustment concepts that would cover temporal reconstruction and reconciliation.

- issue-005: fix_before_release, accept_risk
  - rationale: Coverage keeps this as a distinct high-severity coverage-owned issue while axiology preserves a medium value-alignment caveat. The root is accepted, but its distinctness and severity relative to issue-004 remain disputed, so it needs a closure decision before the ontology advances. Source finding context: OrderLine / Shipment fulfillment coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: OrderLine attributes comment; relations `fulfills` note Source finding context: OrderLine-level fulfillment allocation is missing, so partial shipment coverage is incomplete. Source finding context: For WMS/TMS/ERP integration, fulfillment is not only an order-level state; line quantities may be split, backordered, substituted, or shipped in multiple consignments. Without a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept, the model cannot reconstruct which SKU quantity left in which shipment, weakening reconciliation between order promise, inventory decrement, shipment confirmation, and customer delivery. Source finding context: Add a line-fulfillment concept such as `FulfillmentAllocation` or `ShipmentLine` with refs to `OrderLine`, `Shipment` or `DeliveryLeg`, fulfilled quantity, allocation/pick/ship timestamps, and lifecycle/status for partial, canceled, or backordered quantities. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines or a line is partially fulfilled across multiple shipments, the ontology has no concept to bind the fulfilled quantity to the actual shipment or leg. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because order status, inventory movement, and shipment evidence cannot be traced at the unit where fulfillment actually occurs. Source finding context: The ontology's fulfillment relation is scoped to `Shipment -> Order` and does not include a line-level fulfillment/allocation entity. Source finding context: `OrderLine` has only `sku_ref` and `qty`, with no fulfilled quantity, shipment reference, or delivery-leg reference. Source finding context: The only fulfillment relation is `Shipment fulfills Order`, and its note explicitly excludes OrderLine-level mapping. Source finding context: This prevents the ontology from covering partial shipment and line-level reconciliation scenarios required by the declared integration purpose.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`

Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 기준 문서에서 동일한 quantity_on_hand가 물리적 보유 수량인지, 회계 기준 수량인지, 조정 후 ERP 기준 값인지 구분되지 않으면 동일 필드가 시스템마다 다른 뜻으로 해석된다. 이는 재고 할당, 회계 조정, 운영 조회가 같은 개념을 공유한다는 전제를 약화한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 재고 권위와 주문 할당 의미를 일관되게 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고가 불일치하거나 야간 배치 전후 값을 동시에 다루는 경우 quantity_on_hand의 의미가 시스템별로 달라진다. Source finding context: 통합 참여자가 같은 속성명을 서로 다른 재고 사실로 매핑할 수 있어 주문 할당 가능성, 회계 재고, 조정 대상 수량이 혼동된다. Source finding context: 재고 수량의 권위 체계가 WMS 물리 기준과 ERP 회계 기준으로 분리되어 있는데 온톨로지는 이를 하나의 quantity_on_hand 의미로 명명한다. Source finding context: quantity_on_hand는 '현재 보유 수량'으로 이름 붙어 있지만 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 설명된다. Source finding context: WMS 값은 창고 실물 기준이고 ERP 값은 회계 수량 기준이라고 별도 의미가 명시된다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 의미의 재고 수량이 하나의 속성명으로 통합되어 통합 기준 문서의 의미 정합성을 약화한다. Source finding context: Cross-system quantity and ETA authority coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: InventoryRecord `quantity_on_hand` note; notes `재고 진실`; Shipment `eta` note; notes `ETA` Source finding context: Parallel WMS/ERP and carrier/manual values are described without source-of-truth or provenance concepts. Source finding context: Coverage lens treats single authority and audit evidence as required dimensions when values are managed across systems or manually controlled. Here, both inventory and ETA are explicitly multi-source. Without authority/provenance concepts, downstream integrations cannot know which value governs allocation, accounting, display, or audit, and manual changes cannot be explained after the fact. Source finding context: Add source/provenance and authority concepts, for example `SystemOfRecordPolicy`, `QuantityAssertion`, `EtaEstimate`, and `ManualOverride` with source system, effective time, actor, reason, superseded value, and consuming context. - issue-006: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The issue has no material deliberation conflict and is supported as a medium non-happy-path lifecycle coverage gap. It weakens exception handling and reconciliation completeness, so the lifecycle model should be closed before relying on the ontology as an operational standard. Source finding context: Order and Shipment lifecycle coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: Order `fulfillment_status` values; Shipment `status` values and note; TrackingEvent `event_type` values Source finding context: Lifecycle states stop at happy-path delivery and omit cancellation, exception resolution, return, correction, and archival phases. Source finding context: A logistics fulfillment baseline must cover operational endings and post-final corrections. The current state models treat `delivered` as final while still having carrier exceptions and multi-system updates elsewhere, so real operations such as failed delivery, return-to-sender, cancellation after allocation, shipment reissue, proof-of-delivery correction, and audit closure are outside the conceptual lifecycle. Source finding context: Extend lifecycle coverage with explicit terminal and post-terminal concepts: canceled/backordered for orders or lines; exception/failed_delivery/returned/reissued for shipments; correction or reversal events; and archival/audit-closed states with rules for how post-delivery events affect status. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: Order, freight, and delivery concept consistency for logistics operations. Source finding context: When fulfillment leaves the happy path or a delivered shipment must be corrected, the ontology has no lifecycle concept to represent the condition without overloading existing states. Source finding context: Operations risk is hidden because exceptions and reversals cannot be represented consistently across OMS, TMS, carrier tracking, and ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: Lifecycle enumerations are limited to forward-progress delivery states and define delivered as final. Source finding context: Order and shipment statuses enumerate only normal forward progress to delivered. Source finding context: Carrier tracking can report `exception`, but shipment status has no exception or recovery state. Source finding context: The ontology therefore does not cover cancellation, failed delivery, returns, reissue, correction, or archival lifecycle phases.

- issue-009: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Deliberation resolves the coverage evidence gap and keeps this as a material medium route-composition issue. The part-whole structural error can make route legs inherit whole-shipment meanings and destabilize route/status reasoning, so it must be corrected before the model is used as a common route baseline. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / entities.Shipment and entities.DeliveryLeg Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:40, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:52 Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is declared as a Shipment while its definition allows route segments that are not complete warehouse-to-customer shipments. Source finding context: This is an inter-claim necessary-type conflict: the subtype declaration requires all DeliveryLeg instances to be Shipment instances, while the DeliveryLeg definition permits instances whose origin/destination pattern does not meet the Shipment definition. It matters for WMS/TMS/ERP concept integration because code or mappings that inherit Shipment obligations onto each DeliveryLeg can treat a partial leg as a full customer shipment, weakening status, carrier, and fulfillment reasoning. Source finding context: Make `Shipment` the whole consignment/order movement and model `DeliveryLeg` as a component of Shipment instead of `is_a`, or broaden `Shipment` to mean any physical cargo movement and introduce a separate whole-shipment concept for warehouse-to-customer fulfillment. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Concept baseline for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across order, freight, and delivery concepts. Source finding context: When DeliveryLeg instances such as hub-to-customer are interpreted through the `is_a: Shipment` declaration, they must also satisfy the narrower Shipment definition of warehouse-to-customer movement. Source finding context: The ontology can classify partial route legs as full shipments, making downstream integration rules about shipment status, fulfillment, and delivery scope logically unstable. Source finding context: The ontology uses inheritance for a part-of route segment relationship while keeping the parent definition narrower than the child’s allowed instances. Source finding context: `Shipment` is defined as movement from warehouse to customer. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` is declared `is_a: Shipment`. Source finding context: `DeliveryLeg` permits route segments such as warehouse-to-hub and hub-to-customer, which are not necessarily full warehouse-to-customer movements. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / DeliveryLeg 경로 구성 구조 Source finding context: materialized-input.md: Shipment definition says DeliveryLeg is also a kind of Shipment; DeliveryLeg has leg_seq, from_ref, and to_ref; relations contains only fulfills from Shipment to Order and no parent shipment, route, or next-leg relation. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg has no structural link that groups legs into a shipment route. Source finding context: A sequence number is only meaningful within a containing route or shipment. Because DeliveryLeg is modeled only as an is_a Shipment subtype, each leg inherits shipment-level behavior but lacks a structural edge that says which shipment route it belongs to or which legs precede/follow it. Source finding context: Introduce a parent/composition relation such as Shipment has_many DeliveryLeg, DeliveryLeg belongs_to Shipment, or a separate Route entity with ordered legs; scope leg_seq to that parent. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: Concept reference document for shipment and delivery integration between WMS/TMS/carrier systems. Source finding context: When a delivery has multiple legs, consumers cannot reconstruct the end-to-end route or associate leg-level tracking and locations with one parent shipment. Source finding context: TMS/carrier integration loses a stable structural path for multi-leg delivery state, ETA, and exception handling, even though DeliveryLeg is declared as part of the shipment model. Source finding context: The ontology uses inheritance to relate DeliveryLeg to Shipment but omits the composition or ordering relation required for route-level structure. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg is a route segment with leg_seq/from/to attributes. Source finding context: No explicit relation groups DeliveryLeg instances under a parent shipment or route. Source finding context: The is_a Shipment link makes each leg a kind of shipment but does not provide route containment or adjacency.

- issue-011: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Deliberation keeps this as a material medium semantic type-modeling issue despite coverage's original evidence-ownership limitation. The issue directly conflates segment and whole-shipment meanings, which affects status mapping and fulfillment interpretation. Source finding context: entities.DeliveryLeg.is_a Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: Shipment definition "창고에서 고객으로 가는 물리적 화물 이동. 배송 구간(DeliveryLeg)도 Shipment의 일종이다."; DeliveryLeg definition "화물 경로의 한 구간(예: 창고→허브, 허브→고객)."; relation fulfills from Shipment to Order Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 하위 유형으로 둔 것은 구간과 전체 화물 이동을 혼동한다. Source finding context: 구간은 전체 출하와 부분-전체 관계에 가깝다. 하위 유형으로 두면 창고→허브 구간도 고객 주문을 충족하는 Shipment처럼 해석될 수 있고, 각 leg의 delivered 상태와 전체 shipment/order delivered 상태가 의미적으로 뒤섞인다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg를 Shipment의 subclass가 아니라 Shipment에 속한 route segment로 모델링한다. 예: DeliveryLeg.parent_shipment_ref 또는 Shipment.legs를 두고, fulfills 관계는 전체 Shipment에만 유지한다. Leg 상태는 구간 완료 상태로 별도 정의한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: 화물·배송 개념의 정합성을 제공하여 TMS/OMS/WMS 상태와 관계를 통합하는 목적. Source finding context: 하나의 주문 출하가 여러 구간을 거치거나 중간 허브 구간 상태를 추적할 때 DeliveryLeg가 Shipment로 취급된다. Source finding context: 구간 완료와 전체 배송 완료, 구간 운송과 주문 충족이 같은 의미 계층으로 섞여 상태 매핑과 주문 충족 판단이 부정확해질 수 있다. Source finding context: 배송 구간이라는 부분 개념을 전체 출하 이동의 특수한 종류로 분류했다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 화물 경로의 한 구간으로 정의된다. Source finding context: DeliveryLeg는 is_a: Shipment로 선언되어 전체 Shipment의 의미와 관계를 상속한다. Source finding context: Shipment는 Order를 fulfills하는 관계의 출발점이므로 DeliveryLeg도 주문 충족 주체처럼 해석될 수 있다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-012: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: All lenses support the measurement value/unit root at medium severity. The issue is a root cause for both current aggregation ambiguity and future reinterpretation risk when warehouses, carriers, regions, or rating engines use different unit systems. Source finding context: entities.Sku.attributes.weight, entities.Sku.attributes.dims, entities.Shipment.attributes.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: Sku.weight note "창고별 계량 관례(kg 또는 lb)를 그대로 쓴다."; Sku.dims note "단위는 소스 시스템마다 다르다."; Shipment.total_weight note "구성 OrderLine 중량의 합." Source finding context: weight/dims가 단위 없는 숫자·문자열로 정의되어 Shipment.total_weight의 의미가 안정적인 중량 합계가 될 수 없다. Source finding context: 중량과 치수는 단위가 의미의 일부다. kg와 lb가 같은 number 필드에 섞이면 total_weight라는 파생값은 같은 이름이어도 합산 기준이 불명확하다. WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 운임 산정, 적재 제한, 라벨/운송사 전송에 쓰이는 물류 수치가 의미적으로 비교 불가능해진다. Source finding context: 측정값을 value + unit 구조로 표준화하고 canonical unit 변환 규칙을 둔다. Shipment.total_weight는 normalized_weight 합계인지 원천 측정값인지 명시하고, 가능하면 source weights와 calculated_at/source_basis를 함께 둔다. dims도 length/width/height 각각 value+unit으로 분해한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 주문·재고·화물·배송 개념의 단위 정합성을 보장하는 통합 기준 문서 목적. Source finding context: SKU 중량이나 치수 원천이 kg/lb 또는 서로 다른 길이 단위를 섞어 제공되는 경우 Shipment.total_weight가 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 계산된다. Source finding context: 동일한 total_weight 값이 어떤 단위의 합인지 알 수 없어 운송사, 창고, ERP 간 물류 수치 매핑과 검증이 신뢰하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 측정 단위를 독립된 의미 요소로 모델링하지 않고 소스 시스템 관례에 맡긴다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 number로만 선언되고 kg 또는 lb를 창고별 관례대로 사용한다고 설명된다. Source finding context: Sku.dims도 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다른 문자열로 선언된다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 구성 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되므로 단위가 다른 원천값을 같은 합계 개념으로 만들 수 있다. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml Sku weight/dims and Shipment.total_weight Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:33-38,40-49 Source finding context: 단위가 값과 분리되어 있지 않아 새 창고·운송사·국가 단위계를 추가하면 기존 수치 데이터의 연속성이 깨진다. Source finding context: 확장 관점에서 새 WMS, TMS, 운송사 요율, 국가권역을 붙일 때 중량과 치수는 라벨 발행, 요율 계산, 적재 제한, 통관 확장의 핵심 입력입니다. 현재 숫자와 문자열은 단위 권위를 포함하지 않아 새 단위계를 수용하려면 과거 값의 의미를 재해석하거나 기존 필드를 마이그레이션해야 합니다. Source finding context: 중량과 치수를 value+unit 구조로 모델링하고, 원천 시스템 단위와 표준화 단위를 구분합니다. Shipment.total_weight도 계산 기준 단위와 계산 시점, 원천 SKU 버전을 함께 보존하도록 바꿉니다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합에서 SKU, 화물, 배송 계산의 공통 개념 기준 Source finding context: kg/lb 또는 cm/in가 섞인 창고, 운송사, 국가권역, 요율 엔진을 추가하는 경우 Source finding context: 동일 필드의 숫자가 시스템마다 다른 의미를 가져 기존 데이터와 새 데이터의 비교, 합산, 요율 계산 연속성이 약해집니다. Source finding context: 측정값 개념이 단위와 원천 단위 권위를 속성으로 갖지 않는다. Source finding context: Sku.weight는 숫자만 있고 창고별 kg 또는 lb 관례를 그대로 사용한다고 명시한다. Source finding context: Sku.dims는 단위가 소스 시스템마다 다른 문자열로 저장된다. Source finding context: Shipment.total_weight는 OrderLine 중량의 합으로 저장되지만 단위 또는 변환 기준을 표현하지 않는다. Source finding context: 따라서 새 단위계가 들어오면 기존 필드 의미를 유지한 채 확장하기 어렵고 과거 데이터 해석도 흔들린다.

- issue-013: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: The issue has no material conflict and is supported as a medium canonical mapping need. It is narrower than issue-003 but still a root cause for inconsistent delivered, in_transit, and exception semantics across integrations. Source finding context: entities.TrackingEvent.attributes.event_type and entities.Shipment.attributes.status Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: TrackingEvent.event_type note "캐리어별 상태 코드. 자사 Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다."; integrity_rules "Order.fulfillment_status, Shipment.status, TrackingEvent.event_type는 각 시스템(OMS/TMS/캐리어)이 독립으로 관리한다." Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type과 Shipment.status 사이의 상태 의미가 통합 기준 안에서 정의되지 않는다. Source finding context: 상태 이름에는 의미 전이가 숨어 있다. 예를 들어 TrackingEvent.delivered가 Shipment.status.delivered와 같은 완료 의미인지, 특정 구간의 도착 이벤트인지, 캐리어별 최종 스캔인지 통합 기준이 판단해 주지 않는다. 이 상태 의미를 연동별로 맡기면 같은 이벤트가 통합마다 다른 Shipment 상태를 만들 수 있다. Source finding context: 캐리어 원천 이벤트와 내부 canonical shipment status를 구분하고, 각 이벤트가 상태 전이를 일으키는 조건과 범위를 기준 문서에 둔다. event_type에는 source_carrier_code/source_event_code를 보존하고, canonical_event_type 또는 status_transition 매핑 테이블을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: 배송 상태 모델과 WMS/TMS/ERP/캐리어 통합 상태 의미를 일관되게 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 캐리어 이벤트를 내부 Shipment.status나 Order.fulfillment_status로 반영하는 연동을 둘 이상 구현할 때 각 연동이 자체 매핑을 만든다. Source finding context: delivered, in_transit, exception 같은 상태의 의미가 연동별로 달라져 배송 완료, 예외 처리, 고객 알림 기준이 불일치할 수 있다. Source finding context: 원천 캐리어 이벤트 의미와 내부 Shipment 상태 의미 사이의 canonical 매핑을 온톨로지 밖 연동 구현으로 위임한다. Source finding context: TrackingEvent.event_type은 캐리어별 상태 코드로 정의된다. Source finding context: Shipment.status와의 매핑은 각 연동에서 알아서 한다고 되어 있다. Source finding context: 주문, 화물, 캐리어 상태는 각 시스템이 독립 관리한다고 되어 있어 공통 상태 의미가 기준 문서 안에서 닫히지 않는다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`

### Consensus (6/6, core-axis mode)
- issue-001 (high): Issue-001 is a high-severity material defect: the ontology cannot safely serve as a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline while allocation depends on current or nightly inventory quantity surfaces instead of time-scoped, source-authoritative availability records.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 6/6 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics, structure, logic
  - remaining disagreement: 0/6 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology, coverage
  - issue statement: Shared-root issue across finding-001, finding-006: Inventory allocation depends on non-temporal current/nightly quantity surfaces rather than a time-scoped, source-authoritative availability model. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate inventory authority and allocation timing Source finding context: Target: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:81, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:86, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:111, .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:115. Value authority anchor: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6, statement requires suitability as a WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept standard centered on authority, temporality, units, status models, and logistics operating risk. Source finding context: Nightly ERP-aligned inventory snapshots are misaligned with the ontology's declared purpose as an integration concept standard for inventory authority and time-sensitive allocation. Source finding context: For an integration reference document whose review purpose explicitly centers authority, temporality, and logistics operating risk, making intraday allocation depend on a nightly aggregate accepts a hidden tradeoff: accounting reconciliation convenience overrides operational stock truth at the moment orders are accepted. This weakens the ontology's ability to define which system is authoritative for allocation-time inventory and when a quantity is valid. Source finding context: Separate physical, accounting, and allocatable inventory concepts; add as_of/effective_at timestamps; define the authority for allocation-time availability, and require allocations to reference a current WMS-backed or event-sourced availability record rather than a daily ERP-aligned snapshot. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use the ontology as the WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts, especially authority and temporality. Source finding context: During intraday order allocation, the ontology directs consumers to a daily InventoryAggregate snapshot while operational WMS and accounting ERP quantities may diverge until nightly reconciliation. Source finding context: The reference model can drive oversell, under-allocation, and audit disputes because it does not identify the valid inventory authority or validity time for allocation decisions. Source finding context: The ontology optimizes for nightly ERP reconciliation as the inventory integration authority without modeling allocation-time validity or separating physical, accounting, and allocatable inventory purposes. Source finding context: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is explicitly shared by WMS and ERP, and discrepancies are adjusted to ERP at night. Source finding context: InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a nightly snapshot but is used for intraday allocation. Source finding context: Because the review purpose requires authority and temporality suitability for WMS/TMS/ERP integration, this snapshot allocation rule undermines the declared purpose. Source finding context: InventoryRecord / InventoryAggregate temporal inventory coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: InventoryRecord `quantity_on_hand` note and comment; InventoryAggregate `available_qty` note; integrity rule for allocation Source finding context: Inventory quantities lack effective-time and movement-history coverage despite being used for allocation. Source finding context: Inventory values are time-dependent. The target itself notes that `InventoryRecord` has no `as_of` concept, while allocation reads `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` even for intraday orders although that aggregate is a nightly snapshot. This leaves a coverage gap for historical reconstruction, oversell investigation, reservation accounting, and WMS/ERP reconciliation. Source finding context: Introduce temporal inventory concepts: `InventorySnapshot` with `as_of`, source system, and scope; `InventoryMovement` or `InventoryTransaction` for receipts, picks, shipments, returns, adjustments; and `Reservation`/`Allocation` with effective time and release/consume events. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Inventory and order fulfillment authority for WMS/TMS/ERP integration. Source finding context: When an order is accepted or audited at a prior point in time, the ontology cannot reconstruct available or on-hand inventory from effective timestamps or movement history. Source finding context: The concept baseline cannot support allocation correctness, dispute analysis, or cross-system reconciliation because only current/nightly quantity surfaces are modeled. Source finding context: Inventory is represented as current/nighly aggregate values without temporal source concepts or movement events. Source finding context: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` is a current quantity with no effective timestamp. Source finding context: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` is only a nightly snapshot but is used for intraday allocation decisions. Source finding context: The model lacks inventory movement, reservation, and adjustment concepts that would cover temporal reconstruction and reconciliation.
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as the WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts, especially authority and temporality. / Inventory and order fulfillment authority for WMS/TMS/ERP integration.
  - failure condition: During intraday order allocation, the ontology directs consumers to a daily InventoryAggregate snapshot while operational WMS and accounting ERP quantities may diverge until nightly reconciliation. / When an order is accepted or audited at a prior point in time, the ontology cannot reconstruct available or on-hand inventory from effective timestamps or movement history.
  - impact: The reference model can drive oversell, under-allocation, and audit disputes because it does not identify the valid inventory authority or validity time for allocation decisions. / The concept baseline cannot support allocation correctness, dispute analysis, or cross-system reconciliation because only current/nightly quantity surfaces are modeled.
  - root hypothesis: Inventory allocation depends on non-temporal current/nightly quantity surfaces rather than a time-scoped, source-authoritative availability model.

InventoryAggregate `available_qty` note; integrity rule for allocation`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-001`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-006`, `rel-004`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-004`, `finding-001.cause-001`, `finding-001.cause-002`, `finding-001.cause-003`, `finding-006.cause-001`, `Embedded materialized input: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` and adjacent comment`, `finding-006.cause-002`, `Embedded materialized input: `InventoryAggregate.available_qty` note`, `Embedded materialized input: first integrity rule`, `finding-006.cause-003`, `Embedded materialized input: entities list contains InventoryRecord and InventoryAggregate but no movement/reservation/adjustment entity`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-001`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.logic`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-001`, `rel-006`, `rel-008`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology, coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: The declared purpose depends on clear authority and temporality for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts. Using a nightly InventoryAggregate for intraday allocation while WMS operational quantities and ERP accounting quantities may diverge weakens that purpose because consumers cannot know which inventory fact is authoritative, when it is valid, or how to reconstruct allocation decisions later. This can drive oversell, under-allocation, reconciliation failures, and audit disputes.
  - root cause: The starting point is that inventory availability is modeled as shared current or nightly quantity values rather than as source-governed, time-valid inventory assertions. Because InventoryRecord and InventoryAggregate collapse physical, accounting, and allocatable meanings, downstream allocation has no authoritative allocation-time availability record to reference.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported by both surface findings: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand is shared between WMS and ERP and reconciled nightly, InventoryAggregate.available_qty is a nightly snapshot, and allocation nevertheless reads that aggregate for intraday decisions. Coverage adds that the model lacks effective-time inventory snapshots, movements, reservations, and adjustments, so prior availability cannot be reconstructed for order acceptance, dispute analysis, or WMS/ERP reconciliation.
  - action: Fix this before the next integration-baseline stage by separating physical, accounting, and allocatable inventory concepts; adding as_of/effective_at validity; defining the source authority for each consuming context; and requiring allocations or reservations to reference a current WMS-backed or event-sourced availability record rather than a daily ERP-aligned snapshot. Add temporal InventorySnapshot, InventoryMovement or InventoryTransaction, and Reservation/Allocation concepts so allocation correctness and audit reconstruction have explicit authority and time semantics.

- issue-005 (high): The ontology has a material fulfillment coverage gap: fulfillment is modeled only from Shipment to Order, so it cannot represent which OrderLine quantity was allocated, picked, shipped, canceled, or backordered in a specific shipment or delivery leg.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 2/2 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: coverage, axiology
  - remaining disagreement: 1/2 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: axiology
  - raised by lenses: coverage
  - issue statement: OrderLine-level fulfillment allocation is missing, so partial shipment coverage is incomplete. Source finding context: OrderLine / Shipment fulfillment coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: OrderLine attributes comment; relations `fulfills` note Source finding context: For WMS/TMS/ERP integration, fulfillment is not only an order-level state; line quantities may be split, backordered, substituted, or shipped in multiple consignments. Without a line-level fulfillment/allocation concept, the model cannot reconstruct which SKU quantity left in which shipment, weakening reconciliation between order promise, inventory decrement, shipment confirmation, and customer delivery. Source finding context: Add a line-fulfillment concept such as `FulfillmentAllocation` or `ShipmentLine` with refs to `OrderLine`, `Shipment` or `DeliveryLeg`, fulfilled quantity, allocation/pick/ship timestamps, and lifecycle/status for partial, canceled, or backordered quantities. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts. Source finding context: When one order has multiple lines or a line is partially fulfilled across multiple shipments, the ontology has no concept to bind the fulfilled quantity to the actual shipment or leg. Source finding context: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because order status, inventory movement, and shipment evidence cannot be traced at the unit where fulfillment actually occurs. Source finding context: The ontology's fulfillment relation is scoped to `Shipment -> Order` and does not include a line-level fulfillment/allocation entity. Source finding context: `OrderLine` has only `sku_ref` and `qty`, with no fulfilled quantity, shipment reference, or delivery-leg reference. Source finding context: The only fulfillment relation is `Shipment fulfills Order`, and its note explicitly excludes OrderLine-level mapping. Source finding context: This prevents the ontology from covering partial shipment and line-level reconciliation scenarios required by the declared integration purpose.
  - affected purpose: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts.
  - failure condition: When one order has multiple lines or a line is partially fulfilled across multiple shipments, the ontology has no concept to bind the fulfilled quantity to the actual shipment or leg.
  - impact: Operational reconciliation and exception handling become non-actionable because order status, inventory movement, and shipment evidence cannot be traced at the unit where fulfillment actually occurs.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology's fulfillment relation is scoped to `Shipment -> Order` and does not include a line-level fulfillment/allocation entity.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `OrderLine comment: no reference to actual cargo/segment fulfilling the line`, `relations `fulfills`: Shipment to Order only; note says no OrderLine-level mapping`, `Embedded materialized input: OrderLine attributes comment; relations `fulfills` note`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-005`, `finding-005.cause-001`, `Embedded materialized input: `OrderLine.attributes``, `finding-005.cause-002`, `Embedded materialized input: `relations` entry `fulfills``, `finding-005.cause-003`, `Embedded materialized input header: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline`, `Embedded materialized input: OrderLine omission comment`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-005`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-005`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.structure`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, accept_risk
  - materiality: The declared purpose is a WMS/TMS/ERP integration baseline across order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts. That purpose requires reconciliation at the operational unit where fulfillment actually happens: ordered SKU quantities. If a multi-line order or partially fulfilled line is split across shipments, the model cannot bind fulfilled quantity to shipment evidence, inventory decrement, delivery progress, or customer-facing status, making exception handling non-actionable.
  - root cause: The starting point is the ontology’s fulfillment scope: it provides Shipment -> Order fulfillment but lacks a line-level allocation entity that binds an OrderLine quantity to a Shipment or DeliveryLeg. Because the missing concept is upstream of fulfilled quantity, shipment reference, and lifecycle/status details, downstream order or shipment status cannot recover the line-level facts after the fact.
  - causal path: The causal path is accepted by the reviewing lenses: OrderLine only carries sku_ref and qty, the fulfills relation connects Shipment to Order and explicitly omits OrderLine-level mapping, and therefore partial shipments, backorders, substitutions, or split consignments cannot be reconstructed. This directly produces the observed issue: shipment coverage is incomplete for line-level fulfillment scenarios.
  - action: Add a line-fulfillment concept, such as FulfillmentAllocation or ShipmentLine, before advancing the ontology. It should reference OrderLine and Shipment or DeliveryLeg, carry fulfilled quantity, allocation/pick/ship timestamps, and lifecycle/status for partial, canceled, and backordered quantities. This is required to make order promise, inventory movement, shipment confirmation, and delivery evidence traceable through the same fulfillment unit.
  - unresolved disagreement: Deliberation narrowed but did not fully resolve the issue: coverage keeps the issue distinct and high severity because it blocks operational reconstruction, while axiology accepts the issue and action but preserves a medium severity caveat from the value-alignment lens and its relationship to issue-004.

- issue-007 (high): 부분출하, 다중창고 출고, 백오더 같은 분할 충족 패턴을 지원하려면 현재의 Order 단위 fulfills 구조를 유지한 채로는 충분하지 않으며, 충족 권위를 OrderLine 수량 또는 별도 FulfillmentAllocation/ShipmentLine 단위로 내려야 합니다.

  - issue statement: 부분출하 또는 분할충족을 추가하려면 기존 주문-화물 관계 구조를 바꿔야 한다. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml OrderLine/fulfills shipment mapping Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:25-31,105-109 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준 문서에서 주문 줄과 실제 화물 이동의 연결은 부분출하, 백오더, 대체출고, 다중창고 출고 같은 확장 시나리오의 기본 축입니다. 현재 구조는 주문 단위 fulfills만 제공하므로 이런 시나리오를 추가할 때 새 관계를 옆에 붙이는 수준을 넘어 기존 충족 의미와 상태 집계를 재정의해야 합니다. Source finding context: OrderLine 또는 별도 FulfillmentAllocation/ShipmentLine 개념을 도입해 OrderLine 수량의 어느 부분이 어떤 Shipment/DeliveryLeg로 충족되는지 표현하고, Order.fulfillment_status는 이 세부 충족 상태에서 파생되도록 권위 관계를 정리합니다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: 주문 -> 재고 할당 -> 출하 -> 배송의 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 개념 기준 문서 Source finding context: 부분출하, 다중창고 출고, 백오더처럼 한 주문 또는 한 주문줄이 여러 화물로 나뉘거나 여러 주문줄이 한 화물에 섞이는 경우 Source finding context: 주문줄과 화물의 추적 연속성이 없어 통합 시스템이 어느 재고 할당과 출하가 어떤 고객 주문 품목을 충족했는지 공통 기준으로 판단할 수 없습니다. Source finding context: 충족 관계의 권위 단위가 OrderLine 수량이 아니라 Order 전체로 고정되어 있다. Source finding context: OrderLine은 sku_ref와 qty만 갖고 실제 충족 화물 또는 배송 구간으로 가는 참조가 없다. Source finding context: fulfills 관계는 Shipment에서 Order로만 연결되며 OrderLine 단위 매핑은 두지 않는다고 명시한다. Source finding context: 따라서 부분출하나 분할충족을 추가하면 기존 Order- Shipment 관계의 의미를 유지한 채 확장하기 어렵다.
  - affected purpose: 주문 -> 재고 할당 -> 출하 -> 배송의 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 개념 기준 문서
  - failure condition: 부분출하, 다중창고 출고, 백오더처럼 한 주문 또는 한 주문줄이 여러 화물로 나뉘거나 여러 주문줄이 한 화물에 섞이는 경우
  - impact: 주문줄과 화물의 추적 연속성이 없어 통합 시스템이 어느 재고 할당과 출하가 어떤 고객 주문 품목을 충족했는지 공통 기준으로 판단할 수 없습니다.
  - root hypothesis: 충족 관계의 권위 단위가 OrderLine 수량이 아니라 Order 전체로 고정되어 있다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:25-31`, `materialized-input.md:105-109`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:25-31,105-109`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-010`, `finding-010.cause-001`, `finding-010.cause-002`, `finding-010.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `OrderLine comment: no reference to actual cargo/segment fulfilling the line`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-007.structure`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, accept_risk
  - materiality: 검토 대상의 목적은 주문, 재고 할당, 출하, 배송을 WMS/TMS/ERP 통합 기준으로 연결하는 것입니다. 그러나 현재 모델은 Shipment가 Order 전체를 fulfills하는 관계만 두고 OrderLine 수량이 어떤 화물 또는 배송 구간으로 충족됐는지 표현하지 못합니다. 그 결과 한 주문줄이 여러 출하로 나뉘거나 여러 주문줄이 한 화물에 섞이는 경우, 통합 시스템이 어느 재고 할당과 출하가 어느 고객 주문 품목을 충족했는지 공통 기준으로 판단할 수 없어 목적을 약화합니다.
  - root cause: 문제의 출발점은 충족 관계의 권위 단위가 OrderLine 수량이 아니라 Order 전체로 고정되어 있다는 점입니다. OrderLine에는 sku_ref와 qty만 있고 실제 충족 화물이나 배송 구간으로 가는 참조가 없으며, fulfills 관계도 Shipment에서 Order로만 연결됩니다. 따라서 세부 충족 상태를 원천으로 삼아 Order.fulfillment_status를 파생할 구조가 없습니다.
  - causal path: 먼저 OrderLine이 실제 출하 또는 배송 구간과 연결되지 않기 때문에 품목 수량 단위의 충족 추적이 끊깁니다. 이어서 fulfills 관계가 Shipment에서 Order로만 정의되어 OrderLine 단위 매핑을 배제하므로, 부분 수량이 어떤 Shipment나 DeliveryLeg로 이동했는지 표현할 권위 지점이 없습니다. 이 구조에서는 부분출하나 분할충족을 추가할 때 단순히 새 관계를 옆에 붙이는 것으로는 부족하고, 기존 Order-Shipment 충족 의미와 상태 집계 방식을 재정의해야 합니다.
  - action: 릴리스 전 수정하려면 OrderLine 또는 별도 FulfillmentAllocation/ShipmentLine 개념을 도입해 OrderLine 수량의 어느 부분이 어떤 Shipment 또는 DeliveryLeg로 충족되는지 표현해야 합니다. 또한 Order.fulfillment_status는 이 세부 충족 상태에서 파생되도록 권위 관계를 정리해야 합니다. 이 결정은 부분출하, 다중창고 출고, 백오더 확장 전에 닫혀야 하는 구조적 선행 조건입니다.
  - unresolved disagreement: 진화 관점은 확장 차단 효과 때문에 high severity를 유지하지만, axiology 관점은 동일한 root cause를 수용하면서도 가치 목적만 기준으로 보면 medium severity라는 caveat를 남겼습니다.

- issue-010 (high): Inventory quantity and ETA cannot be trusted as integration baseline values until the ontology models which source governs each value, what context it governs, and what evidence explains overrides or supersession.

  - issue statement: Inventory quantity and ETA authority are left ambiguous because values from WMS, ERP, carriers, and manual operations are not tied to source, governing context, actor, reason, or superseded value. Source finding context: entities.InventoryRecord.attributes.quantity_on_hand Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand note "현재 보유 수량. WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가지며 야간 배치로 ERP 기준에 맞춘다."; notes "재고 진실: WMS가 창고 실물 기준, ERP가 회계 수량 기준." Source finding context: quantity_on_hand가 물리 재고와 회계 재고라는 서로 다른 의미를 같은 속성명으로 합치고 있다. Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 기준 문서에서 동일한 quantity_on_hand가 물리적 보유 수량인지, 회계 기준 수량인지, 조정 후 ERP 기준 값인지 구분되지 않으면 동일 필드가 시스템마다 다른 뜻으로 해석된다. 이는 재고 할당, 회계 조정, 운영 조회가 같은 개념을 공유한다는 전제를 약화한다. Source finding context: InventoryRecord의 재고 수량 개념을 권위와 의미별로 분리한다. 예: WMS 권위의 physical_on_hand, ERP 권위의 book_on_hand/accounting_on_hand, reconciliation_delta 또는 reconciliation_status를 별도 속성으로 두고 각 값에 source_system과 as_of/effective_at을 붙인다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP 통합의 개념 기준 문서로서 재고 권위와 주문 할당 의미를 일관되게 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: WMS 실물 재고와 ERP 회계 재고가 불일치하거나 야간 배치 전후 값을 동시에 다루는 경우 quantity_on_hand의 의미가 시스템별로 달라진다. Source finding context: 통합 참여자가 같은 속성명을 서로 다른 재고 사실로 매핑할 수 있어 주문 할당 가능성, 회계 재고, 조정 대상 수량이 혼동된다. Source finding context: 재고 수량의 권위 체계가 WMS 물리 기준과 ERP 회계 기준으로 분리되어 있는데 온톨로지는 이를 하나의 quantity_on_hand 의미로 명명한다. Source finding context: quantity_on_hand는 '현재 보유 수량'으로 이름 붙어 있지만 WMS와 ERP가 각자 값을 가진다고 설명된다. Source finding context: WMS 값은 창고 실물 기준이고 ERP 값은 회계 수량 기준이라고 별도 의미가 명시된다. Source finding context: 서로 다른 의미의 재고 수량이 하나의 속성명으로 통합되어 통합 기준 문서의 의미 정합성을 약화한다. Source finding context: Cross-system quantity and ETA authority coverage Source finding context: Embedded materialized input: InventoryRecord `quantity_on_hand` note; notes `재고 진실`; Shipment `eta` note; notes `ETA` Source finding context: Parallel WMS/ERP and carrier/manual values are described without source-of-truth or provenance concepts. Source finding context: Coverage lens treats single authority and audit evidence as required dimensions when values are managed across systems or manually controlled. Here, both inventory and ETA are explicitly multi-source. Without authority/provenance concepts, downstream integrations cannot know which value governs allocation, accounting, display, or audit, and manual changes cannot be explained after the fact. Source finding context: Add source/provenance and authority concepts, for example `SystemOfRecordPolicy`, `QuantityAssertion`, `EtaEstimate`, and `ManualOverride` with source system, effective time, actor, reason, superseded value, and consuming context. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: WMS/TMS/ERP integration concept baseline with clear authority for order, inventory, shipment, and delivery values. Source finding context: When WMS and ERP disagree on inventory, or carrier ETA and manual ETA differ, the ontology lacks a concept that identifies the governing value and its evidence. Source finding context: The integration baseline leaves precedence and audit behavior to implementation-specific interpretation, undermining trust in allocation, accounting reconciliation, and customer-facing ETA decisions. Source finding context: The ontology describes multi-source managed values but does not include authority, provenance, or override evidence entities. Source finding context: Inventory quantity is maintained separately by WMS and ERP with different meanings. Source finding context: ETA combines carrier API input and manual operational judgment. Source finding context: No entity or attribute records source-of-truth policy, actor, time, reason, or superseded value for these controlled changes.

final display follows operations judgment`, `Embedded materialized input: InventoryRecord `quantity_on_hand` note; notes `재고 진실`; Shipment `eta` note; notes `ETA``, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-015`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-007`, `rel-007`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-007`, `finding-007.cause-001`, `Embedded materialized input: `InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand` note`, `Embedded materialized input: notes `재고 진실``, `finding-007.cause-002`, `Embedded materialized input: `Shipment.eta` note`, `Embedded materialized input: notes `ETA``, `finding-007.cause-003`, `Embedded materialized input: entities list includes Shipment, TrackingEvent, InventoryRecord, InventoryAggregate, Carrier, but no authority/provenance/override entity`, `finding-015.cause-001`, `finding-015.cause-002`, `finding-015.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md InventoryRecord definition`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-010`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-010`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.logic`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-001`, `rel-006`, `rel-008`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics, coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: The stated purpose is to provide a WMS/TMS/ERP concept baseline with clear authority for inventory, shipment, ETA, and audit-relevant values. That purpose is weakened because WMS physical inventory, ERP accounting inventory, carrier ETA, and manually adjusted ETA are all described as possible values, but the ontology does not say which value controls allocation, accounting reconciliation, customer display, or audit explanation when they differ.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with the ontology treating multi-source controlled values as ordinary attributes instead of modeling source-of-truth, provenance, and override evidence. Because the authority layer is absent, downstream consumers receive values without governing source, consuming context, actor, reason, time, or superseded value.
  - causal path: The evidence path combines two supported findings: InventoryRecord.quantity_on_hand merges WMS physical quantity and ERP accounting quantity under one attribute, while Shipment.eta mixes carrier API values with manual operations judgment. Since no authority, provenance, or override entity records the governing policy and change evidence, precedence is left to each implementation, creating inconsistent interpretations for allocation, accounting, ETA display, and audit.
  - action: Fix this before the next stage by adding explicit authority and provenance concepts such as SystemOfRecordPolicy, QuantityAssertion, EtaEstimate, and ManualOverride. These should capture source system, effective/as-of time, governing consuming context, actor, reason, superseded value, and reconciliation or override status so inventory and ETA consumers can determine the authoritative value and explain why it governs.

- issue-014 (high): OrderLine fulfillment is materially incomplete because the ontology describes shipment composition in terms of order lines but provides no structural way to say which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which Shipment or DeliveryLeg.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 2/2 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: structure, axiology
  - remaining disagreement: 1/2 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: axiology
  - raised by lenses: structure
  - issue statement: OrderLine fulfillment is structurally disconnected from Shipment and DeliveryLeg. Source finding context: logistics-fulfillment-ontology.yaml / OrderLine- Shipment 연결 구조 Source finding context: materialized-input.md: OrderLine attributes only include sku_ref and qty; comment states no reference from the line to the actual fulfilling shipment/leg. relations defines only fulfills from Shipment to Order with note that OrderLine-level mapping is not included. Source finding context: The target’s own comments identify partial-shipment traceability as impossible, and Shipment.total_weight is defined as the sum of constituent OrderLine weights while no structural edge identifies those constituent lines. For a WMS/TMS/ERP integration reference, this weakens the structural basis for reconciling line-level allocation, picking, shipment contents, and delivery evidence. Source finding context: Add a line-level fulfillment/allocation structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, linking OrderLine to Shipment and optionally DeliveryLeg, with shipped/allocated quantity and status-relevant timestamps. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: Concept reference document for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts. Source finding context: When one order is split across multiple shipments or one shipment contains selected lines from multiple orders, the ontology cannot identify which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which physical movement. Source finding context: Integration consumers cannot reliably reconcile OMS line status, WMS pick/pack records, TMS shipment contents, and carrier delivery events at the line level, reducing the ontology’s usefulness as a concept standard. Source finding context: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at order granularity even though shipment composition is described in terms of order lines. Source finding context: OrderLine has no attribute or relation to Shipment or DeliveryLeg. Source finding context: The only explicit fulfillment edge connects Shipment to Order, not Shipment to OrderLine. Source finding context: Shipment composition nevertheless depends on OrderLine membership.
  - affected purpose: Concept reference document for WMS/TMS/ERP integration across order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts.
  - failure condition: When one order is split across multiple shipments or one shipment contains selected lines from multiple orders, the ontology cannot identify which OrderLine quantity was fulfilled by which physical movement.
  - impact: Integration consumers cannot reliably reconcile OMS line status, WMS pick/pack records, TMS shipment contents, and carrier delivery events at the line level, reducing the ontology’s usefulness as a concept standard.
  - root hypothesis: The fulfillment relation is modeled only at order granularity even though shipment composition is described in terms of order lines.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md: OrderLine has sku_ref and qty only, plus an explicit comment that no reference exists to the shipment/leg that fulfilled the line.`, `materialized-input.md: relations contains only fulfills from Shipment to Order and notes that OrderLine-level mapping is omitted.`, `materialized-input.md: Shipment.total_weight note says it is the sum of constituent OrderLine weights.`, `materialized-input.md: OrderLine attributes only include sku_ref and qty; comment states no reference from the line to the actual fulfilling shipment/leg. relations defines only fulfills from Shipment to Order with note that OrderLine-level mapping is not included.`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-019`, `finding-019.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md: OrderLine attributes list only sku_ref and qty.`, `materialized-input.md: OrderLine comment says there is no reference to the actual shipment/leg that fulfilled the line.`, `finding-019.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md: relations entry name fulfills, from Shipment, to Order.`, `materialized-input.md: fulfills note says it is an order-level relation and does not include OrderLine-level mapping.`, `finding-019.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md: Shipment.total_weight note says it is calculated as the sum of constituent OrderLine weights.`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.axiology`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-014`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `OrderLine comment: no reference to actual cargo/segment fulfilling the line`, `relations `fulfills`: Shipment to Order only; note says no OrderLine-level mapping`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-014`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-014.structure`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260718-4065ab62/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, accept_risk
  - materiality: The declared purpose is a WMS/TMS/ERP integration reference across order, inventory, shipment, and delivery concepts. Without line-level fulfillment traceability, consumers cannot reconcile OMS line status, WMS pick/pack records, TMS shipment contents, and carrier delivery events when orders are split, partially shipped, or mixed into shipments with lines from multiple orders.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with fulfillment being modeled only at order granularity. Shipment can fulfill Order, but the ontology omits the allocation concept needed to connect specific OrderLine quantities to the physical shipment or leg that moved them, even though shipment composition depends on those lines.
  - causal path: OrderLine only carries sku_ref and qty and has no relation to Shipment or DeliveryLeg. The explicit fulfills relation connects Shipment to Order and is documented as excluding OrderLine-level mapping. At the same time, Shipment.total_weight is defined from constituent OrderLine weights, so the model depends on line membership while leaving that membership structurally unstated.
  - action: Add a line-level fulfillment or allocation structure, such as ShipmentLine or FulfillmentAllocation, that links OrderLine to Shipment and optionally DeliveryLeg, with shipped or allocated quantity and status-relevant timestamps. This should be closed before the next stage because downstream reconciliation, shipment contents, partial fulfillment, and delivery evidence depend on this mapping.
  - unresolved disagreement: Deliberation accepted the root and narrowed the issue to a structural line-level traceability defect, but preserved axiology's caveat that severity is medium under value-purpose criteria while structure keeps high severity for structural completeness.

- issue-002 (medium): Freight weights, dimensions, and shipment totals are not safe integration values while they remain bare numbers or strings. The ontology must model them as structured measurements with explicit value, unit, source, conversion basis, and relevant package or handling-unit level before it can serve as a unit-consistent WMS/TMS/ERP baseline.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6

  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
